Used to compare skill versions or competing prompt variants. ([QBall-Inc/the-bulwark](https://github.com/QBall-Inc/the-bulwark/blob/HEAD/skills/create-skill/agents/skill-eval-comparator.md) · ★8)
- **skill-eval-grader**: Artifact-based grader for subjective skill evaluations. - **skill-eval-judge**: Blind LLM-as-judge. Compares TWO anonymized candidate responses to a single task against a provided rubric and returns structured per-response scores plus a winner. Receives only the task, the rubric, and the two responses — it knows nothing about skills, A/B arms, tokens, or which response was expected to win. - **skill-eval-reviewer**: Review skill-creator benchmark outputs for a completed iteration.
